- **l1-client-suite-port-spec**: - **Date:** 2026-08-04 - **Status:** PROPOSED. - **Goal (definition of done):** kardamom's CI adopts the test-suite layers and harness patterns that L1 Ethereum execution clients (reth, geth, nethermind, besu, erigon) have converged on, wherever they map onto our architecture. Concretely: (1) the EEST state-test corpus (O(10⁴) EVM cases) runs in-process against `kardamom-engine` on every PR, pinne ([junemartes/kardamom](https://github.com/junemartes/kardamom/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/l1-client-suite-port-spec.md))
- **l1-origin-deposit-derivation-spec**: - **Date:** 2026-07-25 - **Status:** Phases A to E landed. Epochs are the ONLY deposit path: the da-watcher emits one per finalized L1 block, `DepositRef` is retired, and a chain rebuilt from L1 with `kardamom-reconstruct --lockbox` includes its deposits - **Motivated by:** the chain-semantics suite's S8 (`docs/agents/chain-semantics-e2e-suite-spec.md`), which must keep its workload **deposit-free ([junemartes/kardamom](https://github.com/junemartes/kardamom/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/l1-origin-deposit-derivation-spec.md))

- **lazy-experts.data-implementer**: Use when an approved content design settles what an entity is and the work still needs its data files written into the product's own repository, in the project's own schemas. Dispatched by the expert runtime for any `data-implementer`-class expert; also dispatchable directly with a design document and a report to journal into. - **lazy-experts.debugger**: Use when something fails, returns a wrong result, or behaves unexpectedly and nobody knows why yet — the job is to explain the cause and only then fix it. Dispatched by the expert runtime for any `debugger`-class expert; also dispatchable directly with the failure and a working journal. Pick it over the tester when the defect is already known and needs a root cause, and over the implementer when there is no plan to follow because the problem itself is the unknown. Pick it over the planner when file paths, task lists, and test plans would be premature, and over the interpreter when the gaps in the request are already closed. - **lazy-experts.docs-writer**: Use when an approved design settles what the user gets and the product's user-facing documentation still needs writing — straight from the design document, with no plan in between. - **lazy-experts.fiction-writer**: Use when the deliverable is literary text — narrative prose, a scene, dialogue, a lyrical fragment — written from an existing brief or story outline. Dispatched by the expert runtime for any `fiction-writer`-class expert (the only role `/lazy-experts.install` seeds for the sci-fi and fantasy classes); also dispatchable directly with the outline and a target document. Dispatched by the expert runtime for any `implementer`-class expert; also dispatchable directly with a plan and a working journal. - **lazy-experts.interpreter**: Use when a request is too vague to act on — a free-form ask, a rough note, an old doc, a log — and someone needs a gap-free structured brief before any design starts. Dispatched by the expert runtime for any `interpreter`-class expert; also dispatchable directly with the raw input and a target brief path. - **lazy-log.recall**: Use when the user asks why or when one specific thing changed — 'why was X changed?', 'when did we switch to Y?', 'what commit removed Z?'. Pick this over `lazy-log.recall` (ranked individual matches with SHAs) and `lazy-log.timeline` (dated what-happened-when list) when the answer is an explanation rather than a list. - **learn-architect**: Curriculum Architect. Reverse-plans a learning goal into a prerequisite skill DAG with ordered day_index. Use when cold-starting a course or re-planning the unstarted horizon tail after pace drift. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/architect.md))
- **learn-assessor**: Assessor. Builds and grades daily checks (code execution, quiz, explain-back). Use on /learn done or when creating exercises for newly authored nodes. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/assessor.md))
- **learn-author**: Content Author. Turns skill nodes into interactive wiki HTML pages and exercises. Use when filling the rolling horizon buffer or for /learn explain one-shots. Prefer graphify when available. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/author.md))
- **learn-coach**: Coach. Daily-facing voice that assembles today's plan, due reviews, and encouragement without dark patterns. Use on /learn today and for cold-start Day 1 briefing. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/coach.md))
- **learn-conceptual-coach**: Design Phase 2 question ladders and Phase 3a summaries for /learn sessions on conceptual topics (theory, models, mental frameworks, mechanisms). Single-dispatch per phase. Output is structured text the main agent executes interactively. ([honua-dmg/wayfarer](https://github.com/honua-dmg/wayfarer/blob/HEAD/agents/learn-conceptual-coach.md))
- **learn-declarative-coach**: Design Phase 2 question ladders and Phase 3a summaries for /learn sessions on declarative topics (facts, definitions, vocabulary, taxonomies, API surfaces). Single-dispatch per phase. Output is structured text the main agent executes interactively. ([honua-dmg/wayfarer](https://github.com/honua-dmg/wayfarer/blob/HEAD/agents/learn-declarative-coach.md))
- **learn-distiller**: Internal agent. Use craftflow-router for all development tasks. ([aicraft-sdk/craftflow](https://github.com/aicraft-sdk/craftflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/craftflow/agents/learn-distiller.md))
- **learn-domain-expert**: Domain Expert. Critiques skill DAGs and wiki pages for technical accuracy, missing prerequisites, and wrong ordering. Use after Architect proposes a syllabus or when Author content may be incorrect. ([VasiHemanth/learnloop](https://github.com/VasiHemanth/learnloop/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-expert.md))
